    MOUNT_SOURCE=$(findmnt -n -o SOURCE --mountpoint /var/lib/docker)
    MOUNT_SOURCE=$(echo "$MOUNT_SOURCE" | sed -E 's/.*\[([^]]+)\].*/\1/')

    # TEMP WORKAROUND

    umount /var/lib/docker
    if [[ -n "$MOUNT_SOURCE" && -d "$MOUNT_SOURCE" ]]; then
        mv "$MOUNT_SOURCE" "${MOUNT_SOURCE}.orig"
